--- Raw source ---
(size, seed) {
  const next = generator(seed), operations = [], direct = [];
  for (let i = 0; i < size; i++) {
    const bits = next(), add = (bits & 1n) !== 0n,
          operand = Number((bits >> 1n) & 15n) + 1;
    operations.push(add ? new AddOperation(operand)
                        : new MulOperation(operand));
    direct.push([ add, operand ]);
  }
  return [ operations, direct ];
}

--- Raw source ---
(size, seed) {
  const next = generator(seed), operations = [], direct = [];
  for (let i = 0; i < size; i++) {
    const bits = next(), add = (bits & 1n) !== 0n,
          operand = Number((bits >> 1n) & 15n) + 1;
    operations.push(add ? new AddOperation(operand)
                        : new MulOperation(operand));
    direct.push([ add, operand ]);
  }
  return [ operations, direct ];
}

--- Raw source ---
(size, seed) {
  const next = generator(seed), operations = [], direct = [];
  for (let i = 0; i < size; i++) {
    const bits = next(), add = (bits & 1n) !== 0n,
          operand = Number((bits >> 1n) & 15n) + 1;
    operations.push(add ? new AddOperation(operand)
                        : new MulOperation(operand));
    direct.push([ add, operand ]);
  }
  return [ operations, direct ];
}

(direct, repeats, value) {
  let one = 0;
  for (const [add, operand] of direct) {
    const result = add ? (value + operand) | 0 : Math.imul(value, operand);
    one = (one + result) | 0;
  }
  return Math.imul(one, repeats);
}

--- Raw source ---
(operations, repeats, value) {
  let checksum = 0;
  for (let n = 0; n < repeats; n++)
    for (const operation of operations)
      checksum = (checksum + operation.process(value)) | 0;
  return checksum;
}

(operations, repeats, value) {
  let checksum = 0;
  for (let n = 0; n < repeats; n++)
    for (const operation of operations)
      checksum = (checksum + operation.process(value)) | 0;
  return checksum;
}
